### Ran Playwright code
```js
await (async page=>{const ctx=await page.context().browser().newContext({viewport:{width:390,height:844},deviceScaleFactor:2,isMobile:true,hasTouch:true});const p=await ctx.newPage();const out=[];for(const slug of ['gaia','airpods','simon-schuster','motion-connect-2025','caliper','national-park-cards','yomo','highland-harvests','weaponized-innocence','typldn','seek-truth','cellular-symphony','wolff-olins-x-artcenter','independent-lens','peak-energy','whatsapp','optro']){await p.goto('https://micahhoang.com/case-studies/'+slug,{waitUntil:'domcontentloaded'});await p.waitForTimeout(1300);out.push(await p.evaluate(()=>{const rect=e=>{const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:r.x,y:r.y+scrollY,w:r.width,h:r.height}};const info=e=>({tag:e.tagName,name:e.getAttribute('data-framer-name'),cls:e.className,rect:rect(e),width:getComputedStyle(e).width,max:getComputedStyle(e).maxWidth,pad:getComputedStyle(e).padding,display:getComputedStyle(e).display,direction:getComputedStyle(e).flexDirection,columns:getComputedStyle(e).gridTemplateColumns});return {path:location.pathname,overflow:document.documentElement.scrollWidth,text:[...document.querySelectorAll('p')].filter(e=>e.textContent.length>90&&e.getBoundingClientRect().width>0).map(e=>({text:e.textContent.slice(0,100),...info(e),wrap:getComputedStyle(e).textWrap,parents:[e.parentElement,e.parentElement.parentElement,e.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement].map(info)})),videos:[...document.querySelectorAll('video')].filter(e=>e.getBoundingClientRect().width>0).map(e=>({src:e.currentSrc||e.getAttribute('src'),deferred:e.dataset.videoSourceDeferred,auto:e.autoplay,muted:e.muted,inline:e.playsInline,controls:e.controls,paused:e.paused,ready:e.readyState,...info(e),parents:[e.parentElement,e.parentElement.parentElement].map(info)})),media:[...document.querySelectorAll('img,video')].filter(e=>e.getBoundingClientRect().width>50&&!e.closest('nav,[data-framer-name="SectionNextProject"]')).map(e=>({alt:e.getAttribute('alt')||e.getAttribute('aria-label'),src:e.getAttribute('src'),...info(e),parents:[e.parentElement,e.parentElement.parentElement].map(info)})),iframes:[...document.querySelectorAll('iframe')].map(e=>({src:e.src,...info(e)}))}}));}await ctx.close();return out}
)(page);
```
